# decanter/data/oh_h2o_vacuum_lines.txt -- curated VACUUM line table for per-frame wavelength anchoring
# (decanter.wavecal.LineTable).  Wavelengths in Angstrom, vacuum, lambda = 1e8 / nu.
# Range 8900-13800 A (WINERED WIDE / HIRES-Y / HIRES-J).  Built 2026-10-20 by
# scratchpad/wl_impl/build_linetable.py from the ThAr-free wavelength study (scratchpad/thar_free).
#
# PROVENANCE
#  * HITRAN (HITRAN2020; Gordon et al. 2022, JQSRT 277, 107949), fetched 2026-10-20T23:51Z with
#    hapi 1.3.0.0 fetch() from hitran.org: OH (mol 13, iso 1), H2O (mol 1, iso 1), O2 (mol 7, iso 1),
#    7000-11500 cm-1.  Line positions nu [cm-1], intensities S [cm/molecule, 296 K], gamma_air,
#    delta_air [cm-1/atm] are copied verbatim (exactly coincident components merged: summed S,
#    the strongest member's other parameters).
#  * OH emission intensities I_rel are a MODEL (not HITRAN): HITRAN g'A x two-temperature
#    rotational populations (T1 = 193.6 K, T2 = 5299 K, f_hot = 0.0051) x per-v'
#    populations P(v') = 2:1.596, 3:1.000, 4:0.627, 5:0.370, 6:0.204, 7:0.204, 8:0.178, 9:0.102 (relative to v'=3;
#    v'=2 extrapolated), fitted to the Oliva et al. 2015 GIANO doublet fluxes (A&A 581, A47;
#    VizieR J/A+A/581/A47 table1), 0.15 dex rms.  Normalised to the brightest v'<=9 line
#    (lambda 14186.17 A, 2-0 RR(1.5)).  Only v' <= 9, I_rel >= 3e-5.
#  * lam_oliva: Oliva+2015 table1 position (computed from the Bernath & Colin 2009 molecular
#    constants, 3 decimals) of the matched main-branch component (|HITRAN - Oliva| < 0.5 km/s), else nan.
#    HITRAN - Oliva over 440 matched components: median -20.6 m/s, robust sd
#    43.8 m/s (per v': 3:-21, 4:-19, 5:-51, 6:-18, 7:-51, 8:+25, 9:+15 m/s).
#  * Absorber depth / blend columns are a MODEL at reference conditions: Lorentz lines (HITRAN S,
#    gamma_air, delta_air) at effective pressures P_eff(H2O) = 0.60 atm, P_eff(O2) = 0.38 atm,
#    columns N(H2O) = 4 mm PWV x airmass = 1.336e22 cm-2, N(O2) = 4.5e24 cm-2, convolved with a
#    Gaussian LSF of R = 60000 (WINERED HIRES measured) or 25000 (WIDE).  depthR = 1 - T at the
#    line centre; blendR = max over other H2O/O2 components within 2.5 FWHM of
#    tau_peak,j exp(-dv^2/2 sigma^2) / tau_peak,k.  Absorbers kept if depth >= 0.03 and
#    blend <= 0.2 at either R (S N_ref > 1e-4 cm-1); i.e. anchor CANDIDATES only, not a
#    complete telluric model.  Pressure shifts are NOT applied to the
#    listed positions: the anchor applies lambda = 1e8 / (nu + delta_air P_eff) at run time.
#
# FORMAT: two record types, whitespace separated, selected by the first token.
#  OH  lam_vac I_rel band branch Omega' J parity group nu_err lam_oliva
#      band = v'-v''; branch = HITRAN DeltaN DeltaJ label (PP/QQ/RR main, others satellite);
#      Omega' = upper-state Omega (3/2 = F1, 1/2 = F2); J = lower-state J; parity = HITRAN e/f
#      label; group = Lambda-doublet id (rows sharing it are the two parity components of one
#      rotational line); nu_err = HITRAN position-uncertainty code.
#  H2O|O2  nu0 S gamma_air delta_air depth60 blend60 depth25 blend25   (lambda_vac = 1e8 / nu0)
# Counts: OH 2412 components (1220 doublet groups); H2O 2917; O2 103.
